<content lines="1-422">
  1 | # 🤖 BobCI - AI-Powered Pull Request Intelligence
  2 | 
  3 | BobCI is an intelligent Pull Request analysis system powered by IBM Bob that automatically reviews your GitHub PRs and provides comprehensive insights including impact analysis, security scanning, test generation, documentation, and beginner-friendly explanations.
  4 | 
  5 | ## ✨ Features
  6 | 
  7 | - **🎯 Impact Analysis** - Identifies which files are affected and potential breaking changes
  8 | - **🔒 Security Scanning** - Detects vulnerabilities, hardcoded secrets, and security risks
  9 | - **🧪 Test Generation** - Auto-generates test cases covering happy paths, edge cases, and security scenarios
 10 | - **📚 Documentation** - Creates comprehensive documentation for all changed functions
 11 | - **🎓 Junior Developer Guide** - Explains changes in simple terms with analogies and learning resources
 12 | 
 13 | ## 🏗️ Architecture
 14 | 
 15 | ```
 16 | ┌─────────────────┐
 17 | │  GitHub PR      │
 18 | │  (Webhook)      │
 19 | └────────┬────────┘
 20 |          │
 21 |          ▼
 22 | ┌─────────────────┐      ┌──────────────┐
 23 | │  FastAPI        │─────▶│  IBM Bob     │
 24 | │  Backend        │◀─────│  Shell       │
 25 | └────────┬────────┘      └──────────────┘
 26 |          │
 27 |          ▼
 28 | ┌─────────────────┐      ┌──────────────┐
 29 | │  SQLite         │      │  Next.js     │
 30 | │  Database       │◀─────│  Frontend    │
 31 | └─────────────────┘      └──────────────┘
 32 | ```
 33 | 
 34 | ## 🚀 Quick Start
 35 | 
 36 | ### Prerequisites
 37 | 
 38 | - Python 3.9+
 39 | - Node.js 18+
 40 | - IBM Bob Shell installed and accessible
 41 | - GitHub Personal Access Token
 42 | 
 43 | ### Installation
 44 | 
 45 | 1. **Clone the repository**
 46 | ```bash
 47 | git clone <your-repo-url>
 48 | cd bobci
 49 | ```
 50 | 
 51 | 2. **Run the setup script**
 52 | ```bash
 53 | # On Unix/Linux/Mac
 54 | bash setup.sh
 55 | 
 56 | # On Windows
 57 | .\setup.bat
 58 | ```
 59 | 
 60 | Or follow the manual setup below:
 61 | 
 62 | ### Manual Setup
 63 | 
 64 | #### Backend Setup
 65 | 
 66 | 1. **Create Python virtual environment**
 67 | ```bash
 68 | cd backend
 69 | python -m venv venv
 70 | 
 71 | # Activate virtual environment
 72 | # On Windows:
 73 | venv\Scripts\activate
 74 | # On Unix/Linux/Mac:
 75 | source venv/bin/activate
 76 | ```
 77 | 
 78 | 2. **Install dependencies**
 79 | ```bash
 80 | pip install -r requirements.txt
 81 | ```
 82 | 
 83 | 3. **Configure environment variables**
 84 | ```bash
 85 | cp .env.example .env
 86 | ```
 87 | 
 88 | Edit `.env` and set:
 89 | ```env
 90 | GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET=your_webhook_secret_here
 91 | DATABASE_URL=sqlite:///./bobci.db
 92 | BOB_SHELL_PATH=bob
 93 | WATSONX_API_KEY=your_watsonx_api_key_here  # Optional
 94 | WATSONX_PROJECT_ID=your_project_id_here    # Optional
 95 | FRONTEND_URL=http://localhost:3000
 96 | ```
 97 | 
 98 | 4. **Start the backend server**
 99 | ```bash
100 | python main.py
101 | # Or with uvicorn:
102 | uvicorn main:app --reload --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000
103 | ```
104 | 
105 | The API will be available at `http://localhost:8000`
106 | 
107 | #### Frontend Setup
108 | 
109 | 1. **Install dependencies**
110 | ```bash
111 | cd frontend
112 | npm install
113 | ```
114 | 
115 | 2. **Start the development server**
116 | ```bash
117 | npm run dev
118 | ```
119 | 
120 | The frontend will be available at `http://localhost:3000`
121 | 
122 | ## 📖 Usage
123 | 
124 | ### 1. Connect a GitHub Repository
125 | 
126 | 1. Navigate to `http://localhost:3000/setup`
127 | 2. Create a GitHub Personal Access Token:
128 |    - Go to https://github.com/settings/tokens/new
129 |    - Select `repo` scope
130 |    - Generate token
131 | 3. Fill in the form:
132 |    - **GitHub Owner**: e.g., `facebook`
133 |    - **Repository Name**: e.g., `react`
134 |    - **Personal Access Token**: Your GitHub token
135 |    - **Webhook Secret**: Auto-generated (or create your own)
136 | 4. Click "Connect Repository"
137 | 
138 | ### 2. Configure GitHub Webhook
139 | 
140 | 1. Go to your repository on GitHub
141 | 2. Navigate to **Settings** → **Webhooks** → **Add webhook**
142 | 3. Configure:
143 |    - **Payload URL**: `http://your-server:8000/webhook/github`
144 |    - **Content type**: `application/json`
145 |    - **Secret**: Use the webhook secret from step 1
146 |    - **Events**: Select "Pull requests"
147 | 4. Click "Add webhook"
148 | 
149 | **For local development**, use [ngrok](https://ngrok.com) to expose your local server:
150 | ```bash
151 | ngrok http 8000
152 | # Use the ngrok URL as your webhook URL
153 | ```
154 | 
155 | ### 3. Open a Pull Request
156 | 
157 | When you open a PR on your connected repository:
158 | 
159 | 1. BobCI receives the webhook
160 | 2. IBM Bob analyzes the PR (30-60 seconds)
161 | 3. Five detailed reports are posted as PR comments:
162 |    - Impact Analysis
163 |    - Security Scan
164 |    - Test Cases
165 |    - Documentation
166 |    - Junior Developer Guide
167 | 4. View all reports in the BobCI dashboard
168 | 
169 | ## 🎨 Dashboard Features
170 | 
171 | ### Main Dashboard
172 | - **Stats Overview**: Total PRs, critical issues, vulnerabilities, tests generated
173 | - **Filter by Risk**: Low, Medium, High, Critical
174 | - **Search**: Find PRs by title or repository
175 | - **Auto-refresh**: Updates every 30 seconds
176 | 
177 | ### PR Detail Page
178 | - **Tabbed Interface**: Switch between 5 report types
179 | - **Real-time Updates**: Auto-refreshes while analyzing
180 | - **Copy to Clipboard**: Copy code snippets and test cases
181 | - **GitHub Integration**: Direct link to PR on GitHub
182 | 
183 | ## 🛠️ Tech Stack
184 | 
185 | ### Backend
186 | - **FastAPI** - Modern Python web framework
187 | - **SQLAlchemy** - SQL toolkit and ORM
188 | - **SQLite** - Lightweight database
189 | - **IBM Bob Shell** - AI-powered code analysis
190 | - **GitHub API** - Repository and PR integration
191 | 
192 | ### Frontend
193 | - **Next.js 14** - React framework with SSR
194 | - **Tailwind CSS** - Utility-first CSS framework
195 | - **Lucide React** - Beautiful icon library
196 | - **Axios** - HTTP client
197 | - **date-fns** - Date formatting
198 | 
199 | ## 📁 Project Structure
200 | 
201 | ```
202 | bobci/
203 | ├── backend/
204 | │   ├── main.py              # FastAPI application
205 | │   ├── models.py            # Database models
206 | │   ├── database.py          # Database configuration
207 | │   ├── github_client.py     # GitHub API wrapper
208 | │   ├── bob_analyzer.py      # IBM Bob integration
209 | │   ├── webhook_handler.py   # Webhook processing

212 | │
213 | ├── frontend/

218 | │   ├── components/
219 | │   │   ├── Navbar.js
220 | │   │   ├── PRCard.js
221 | │   │   ├── ImpactReport.js
222 | │   │   ├── SecurityReport.js
223 | │   │   ├── TestReport.js
224 | │   │   ├── DocsReport.js
225 | │   │   └── JuniorGuide.js
226 | │   ├── lib/
227 | │   │   └── api.js           # API client
228 | │   ├── styles/

230 | │   ├── package.json

233 | │
234 | └── bob_sessions/            # Bob session storage
235 | ```
236 | 
237 | ## 🔧 Configuration
238 | 
239 | ### Environment Variables
240 | 
241 | **Backend (.env)**
242 | ```env
243 | # GitHub webhook secret for signature verification
244 | GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET=your_secret
245 | 
246 | # Database connection string
247 | DATABASE_URL=sqlite:///./bobci.db
248 | 
249 | # IBM Bob Shell path
250 | BOB_SHELL_PATH=bob
251 | 
252 | # Optional: Watsonx.ai API credentials
253 | WATSONX_API_KEY=your_key
254 | WATSONX_PROJECT_ID=your_project
255 | 
256 | # Frontend URL for CORS
257 | FRONTEND_URL=http://localhost:3000
258 | ```
259 | 
260 | ### IBM Bob Configuration
261 | 
262 | BobCI uses IBM Bob Shell for AI-powered analysis. Ensure Bob is installed and accessible:
263 | 
264 | ```bash
265 | # Test Bob installation
266 | bob --version
267 | 
268 | # If Bob is not in PATH, specify full path in .env
269 | BOB_SHELL_PATH=/usr/local/bin/bob
270 | ```
271 | 
272 | ## 🧪 Testing
273 | 
274 | ### Demo Script
275 | 
276 | Test BobCI without a real GitHub PR:
277 | 
278 | ```bash
279 | cd bobci
280 | bash demo_pr.sh
281 | ```
282 | 
283 | This script:
284 | 1. Creates a test repository
285 | 2. Generates a sample PR with intentional issues
286 | 3. Sends a webhook payload to your local server
287 | 4. Triggers the full analysis pipeline
288 | 
289 | ### Manual Testing
290 | 
291 | 1. **Test Backend API**
292 | ```bash
293 | curl http://localhost:8000/health
294 | curl http://localhost:8000/api/stats
295 | ```
296 | 
297 | 2. **Test Frontend**
298 | - Navigate to `http://localhost:3000`
299 | - Check dashboard loads
300 | - Test repository setup flow
301 | 
302 | 3. **Test Webhook**
303 | ```bash
304 | curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/webhook/github \
305 |   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
306 |   -H "X-GitHub-Event: pull_request" \
307 |   -d @test_payload.json
308 | ```
309 | 
310 | ## 🐛 Troubleshooting
311 | 
312 | ### Backend Issues
313 | 
314 | **Database errors**
315 | ```bash
316 | # Reset database
317 | rm backend/bobci.db
318 | python backend/main.py  # Will recreate tables
319 | ```
320 | 
321 | **Bob Shell not found**
322 | ```bash
323 | # Check Bob installation
324 | which bob
325 | # Update BOB_SHELL_PATH in .env
326 | ```
327 | 
328 | **Port already in use**
329 | ```bash
330 | # Change port in main.py or use:
331 | uvicorn main:app --port 8001
332 | ```
333 | 
334 | ### Frontend Issues
335 | 
336 | **Module not found**
337 | ```bash
338 | cd frontend
339 | rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json
340 | npm install
341 | ```
342 | 
343 | **API connection failed**
344 | - Ensure backend is running on port 8000
345 | - Check CORS settings in backend/main.py
346 | - Verify next.config.js proxy configuration
347 | 
348 | ### Webhook Issues
349 | 
350 | **Webhook not triggering**
351 | - Verify webhook URL is accessible from GitHub
352 | - Check webhook secret matches in both places
353 | - Review GitHub webhook delivery logs
354 | - Use ngrok for local testing
355 | 
356 | **Signature verification failed**
357 | - Ensure GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET matches GitHub settings
358 | - Check webhook payload format
359 | 
360 | ## 📊 Database Schema
361 | 
362 | ### Repository
363 | - `id`: Primary key
364 | - `github_owner`: Repository owner
365 | - `github_repo`: Repository name
366 | - `github_token`: Personal access token
367 | - `webhook_secret`: Webhook verification secret
368 | - `created_at`: Timestamp
369 | - `is_active`: Boolean flag
370 | 
371 | ### PullRequest
372 | - `id`: Primary key
373 | - `repository_id`: Foreign key
374 | - `pr_number`: PR number
375 | - `pr_title`: PR title
376 | - `pr_author`: Author username
377 | - `pr_url`: GitHub URL
378 | - `base_branch`: Target branch
379 | - `head_branch`: Source branch
380 | - `diff_content`: Full git diff
381 | - `status`: pending/analyzing/complete/failed
382 | - `risk_level`: low/medium/high/critical
383 | - `created_at`: Timestamp
384 | - `analyzed_at`: Analysis completion time
385 | 
386 | ### AnalysisReport
387 | - `id`: Primary key
388 | - `pull_request_id`: Foreign key
389 | - `report_type`: impact/security/tests/documentation/junior_guide
390 | - `content`: JSON report data
391 | - `github_comment_id`: Posted comment ID
392 | - `created_at`: Timestamp
393 |

  2 | from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
  3 | from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
  4 | from sqlalchemy import func
  5 | from typing import List, Dict, Any
  6 | import json
  7 | import os
  8 | from dotenv import load_dotenv
  9 | 
 10 | from database import get_db, init_db
 11 | from models import Repository, PullRequest, AnalysisReport
 12 | from github_client import GitHubClient
 13 | from webhook_handler import handle_pull_request_event
 14 | 
 15 | load_dotenv()
 16 | 
 17 | app = FastAPI(
 18 |     title="BobCI API",
 19 |     description="AI-powered Pull Request intelligence system using IBM Bob",
 20 |     version="1.0.0"
 21 | )
 22 | 
 23 | app.add_middleware(
 24 |     CORSMiddleware,
 25 |     allow_origins=[os.getenv("FRONTEND_URL", "http://localhost:3000")],
 26 |     allow_credentials=True,
 27 |     allow_methods=["*"],
 28 |     allow_headers=["*"],
 29 | )
 30 | 
 31 | github_client = GitHubClient()
 32 | 
 33 | @app.on_event("startup")
 34 | async def startup_event():
 35 |     """Initialize database on application startup."""
 36 |     init_db()
 37 |     print("✅ Database initialized")
 38 |     print("🚀 BobCI API is running")
 39 | 
 40 | @app.get("/")
 41 | async def root():
 42 |     """Root endpoint with API information."""
 43 |     return {
 44 |         "name": "BobCI API",
 45 |         "version": "1.0.0",
 46 |         "description": "AI-powered Pull Request intelligence system",
 47 |         "endpoints": {
 48 |             "webhook": "/webhook/github",
 49 |             "repositories": "/api/repositories",
 50 |             "pull_requests": "/api/pull-requests",
 51 |             "stats": "/api/stats"
 52 |         }
 53 |     }
 54 | 
 55 | @app.post("/webhook/github")
 56 | async def github_webhook(
 57 |     request: Request,
 58 |     background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
 59 |     db: Session = Depends(get_db)
 60 | ):
 61 |     """
 62 |     Handle incoming GitHub webhook events.
 63 |     Verifies HMAC signature and processes pull request events.
 64 |     """
 65 |     signature = request.headers.get("X-Hub-Signature-256", "")
 66 |     event_type = request.headers.get("X-GitHub-Event", "")
 67 |     
 68 |     body = await request.body()
 69 |     
 70 |     webhook_secret = os.getenv("GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
 71 |     
 72 |     if webhook_secret and not github_client.verify_webhook_signature(body, signature, webhook_secret):
 73 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid webhook signature")
 74 |     
 75 |     try:
 76 |         payload = json.loads(body)
 77 |     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 78 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid JSON payload")
 79 |     
 80 |     if event_type == "pull_request":
 81 |         result = handle_pull_request_event(payload, db, background_tasks)
 82 |         return result
 83 |     
 84 |     return {"status": "ignored", "event": event_type}
 85 | 
 86 | @app.get("/api/repositories")
 87 | async def get_repositories(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
 88 |     """
 89 |     Get all connected repositories.
 90 |     """
 91 |     repositories = db.query(Repository).filter(Repository.is_active == True).all()
 92 |     
 93 |     return [
 94 |         {
 95 |             "id": repo.id,
 96 |             "owner": repo.github_owner,
 97 |             "repo": repo.github_repo,
 98 |             "created_at": repo.created_at.isoformat(),
 99 |             "is_active": repo.is_active,
100 |             "pr_count": len(repo.pull_requests)
101 |         }
102 |         for repo in repositories
103 |     ]
104 | 
105 | @app.post("/api/repositories")
106 | async def add_repository(
107 |     data: Dict[str, str],
108 |     db: Session = Depends(get_db)
109 | ):
110 |     """
111 |     Connect a new GitHub repository.
112 |     
113 |     Expected body:
114 |     {
115 |         "owner": "facebook",
116 |         "repo": "react",
117 |         "github_token": "ghp_...",
118 |         "webhook_secret": "random_secret"
119 |     }
120 |     """
121 |     owner = data.get("owner")
122 |     repo = data.get("repo")
123 |     github_token = data.get("github_token")
124 |     webhook_secret = data.get("webhook_secret")
125 |     
126 |     if not all([owner, repo, github_token, webhook_secret]):
127 |         raise HTTPException(
128 |             status_code=400,
129 |             detail="Missing required fields: owner, repo, github_token, webhook_secret"
130 |         )
131 |     
132 |     existing = db.query(Repository).filter(
133 |         Repository.github_owner == owner,
134 |         Repository.github_repo == repo
135 |     ).first()
136 |     
137 |     if existing:
138 |         existing.github_token = github_token
139 |         existing.webhook_secret = webhook_secret
140 |         existing.is_active = True
141 |         db.commit()
142 |         db.refresh(existing)
143 |         repository = existing
144 |     else:
145 |         repository = Repository(
146 |             github_owner=owner,
147 |             github_repo=repo,
148 |             github_token=github_token,
149 |             webhook_secret=webhook_secret,
150 |             is_active=True
151 |         )
152 |         db.add(repository)
153 |         db.commit()
154 |         db.refresh(repository)
155 |     
156 |     return {
157 |         "id": repository.id,
158 |         "owner": repository.github_owner,
159 |         "repo": repository.github_repo,
160 |         "created_at": repository.created_at.isoformat(),
161 |         "webhook_url": f"{os.getenv('BACKEND_URL', 'http://localhost:8000')}/webhook/github",
162 |         "message": "Repository connected successfully"
163 |     }
164 | 
165 | @app.get("/api/pull-requests")
166 | async def get_pull_requests(
167 |     repo_id: int = None,
168 |     status: str = None,
169 |     risk_level: str = None,
170 |     db: Session = Depends(get_db)
171 | ):
172 |     """
173 |     Get all pull requests with optional filters.
174 |     
175 |     Query params:
176 |     - repo_id: Filter by repository ID
177 |     - status: Filter by status (pending, analyzing, complete, failed)
178 |     - risk_level: Filter by risk level (low, medium, high, critical)
179 |     """
180 |     query = db.query(PullRequest)
181 |     
182 |     if repo_id:
183 |         query = query.filter(PullRequest.repository_id == repo_id)
184 |     
185 |     if status:
186 |         query = query.filter(PullRequest.status == status)
187 |     
188 |     if risk_level:
189 |         query = query.filter(PullRequest.risk_level == risk_level)
190 |     
191 |     pull_requests = query.order_by(PullRequest.created_at.desc()).all()
192 |     
193 |     return [
194 |         {
195 |             "id": pr.id,
196 |             "repository_id": pr.repository_id,
197 |             "repository_name": f"{pr.repository.github_owner}/{pr.repository.github_repo}",
198 |             "pr_number": pr.pr_number,
199 |             "pr_title": pr.pr_title,
200 |             "pr_author": pr.pr_author,
201 |             "pr_url": pr.pr_url,
202 |             "base_branch": pr.base_branch,
203 |             "head_branch": pr.head_branch,
204 |             "status": pr.status,
205 |             "risk_level": pr.risk_level,
206 |             "created_at": pr.created_at.isoformat(),
207 |             "analyzed_at": pr.analyzed_at.isoformat() if pr.analyzed_at else None,
208 |             "report_count": len(pr.analysis_reports)
209 |         }
210 |         for pr in pull_requests
211 |     ]
212 | 
213 | @app.get("/api/pull-requests/{pr_id}")
214 | async def get_pull_request(pr_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
215 |     """
216 |     Get detailed information about a specific pull request including all reports.
217 |     """
218 |     pr = db.query(PullRequest).filter(PullRequest.id == pr_id).first()
219 |     
220 |     if not pr:
221 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Pull request not found")
222 |     
223 |     reports = {}
224 |     for report in pr.analysis_reports:
225 |         try:
226 |             reports[report.report_type] = json.loads(report.content)
227 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
228 |             reports[report.report_type] = {"error": "Failed to parse report"}
229 |     
230 |     return {
231 |         "id": pr.id,
232 |         "repository_id": pr.repository_id,
233 |         "repository_name": f"{pr.repository.github_owner}/{pr.repository.github_repo}",
234 |         "pr_number": pr.pr_number,
235 |         "pr_title": pr.pr_title,
236 |         "pr_author": pr.pr_author,
237 |         "pr_url": pr.pr_url,
238 |         "base_branch": pr.base_branch,
239 |         "head_branch": pr.head_branch,
240 |         "status": pr.status,
241 |         "risk_level": pr.risk_level,
242 |         "created_at": pr.created_at.isoformat(),
243 |         "analyzed_at": pr.analyzed_at.isoformat() if pr.analyzed_at else None,
244 |         "diff_content": pr.diff_content,
245 |         "reports": reports
246 |     }
247 | 
248 | @app.get("/api/stats")
249 | async def get_stats(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
250 |     """
251 |     Get dashboard statistics.
252 |     """
253 |     total_prs = db.query(func.count(PullRequest.id)).scalar()
254 |     
255 |     prs_by_risk = db.query(
256 |         PullRequest.risk_level,
257 |         func.count(PullRequest.id)
258 |     ).filter(
259 |         PullRequest.risk_level.isnot(None)
260 |     ).group_by(PullRequest.risk_level).all()
261 |     
262 |     risk_counts = {
263 |         "low": 0,
264 |         "medium": 0,
265 |         "high": 0,
266 |         "critical": 0
267 |     }
268 |     
269 |     for risk_level, count in prs_by_risk:
270 |         if risk_level in risk_counts:
271 |             risk_counts[risk_level] = count
272 |     
273 |     total_vulnerabilities = 0
274 |     total_tests = 0
275 |     security_scores = []
276 |     
277 |     security_reports = db.query(AnalysisReport).filter(
278 |         AnalysisReport.report_type == "security"
279 |     ).all()
280 |     
281 |     for report in security_reports:
282 |         try:
283 |             content = json.loads(report.content)
284 |             vulnerabilities = content.get("vulnerabilities", [])
285 |             total_vulnerabilities += len(vulnerabilities)
286 |             
287 |             score = content.get("overall_score", 0)
288 |             if score > 0:
289 |                 security_scores.append(score)
290 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
291 |             pass
292 |     
293 |     test_reports = db.query(AnalysisReport).filter(
294 |         AnalysisReport.report_type == "tests"
295 |     ).all()
296 |     
297 |     for report in test_reports:
298 |         try:
299 |             content = json.loads(report.content)
300 |             cases = content.get("cases", [])
301 |             total_tests += len(cases)
302 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
303 |             pass
304 |     
305 |     avg_security_score = sum(security_scores) / len(security_scores) if security_scores else 0
306 |     
307 |     return {
308 |         "total_prs": total_prs,
309 |         "prs_by_risk": risk_counts,
310 |         "total_vulnerabilities": total_vulnerabilities,
311 |         "total_tests_generated": total_tests,
312 |         "average_security_score": round(avg_security_score, 1),
313 |         "repositories_connected": db.query(func.count(Repository.id)).filter(
314 |             Repository.is_active == True
315 |         ).scalar()
316 |     }
317 | 
318 | @app.delete("/api/repositories/{repo_id}")
319 | async def delete_repository(repo_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
320 |     """
321 |     Deactivate a repository.
322 |     """
323 |     repository = db.query(Repository).filter(Repository.id == repo_id).first()
324 |     
325 |     if not repository:
326 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Repository not found")
327 |     
328 |     repository.is_active = False
329 |     db.commit()
330 |     
331 |     return {"message": "Repository deactivated successfully"}
332 | 
333 | @app.get("/health")
334 | async def health_check():
335 |     """Health check endpoint."""
336 |     return {"status": "healthy", "service": "BobCI API"}
337 | 
338 | if __name__ == "__main__":
339 |     import uvicorn
340 |     uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000)
341 | 
342 | # Made with Bob
</content>

  1 | import subprocess
  2 | import json
  3 | import os
  4 | from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
  5 | from dotenv import load_dotenv
  6 | 
  7 | load_dotenv()
  8 | 
  9 | class BobAnalyzer:
 10 |     def __init__(self):
 11 |         self.bob_shell_path = os.getenv("BOB_SHELL_PATH", "bob")
 12 | 
 13 |     def analyze_pull_request(self, diff_content: str, repo_path: str = ".") -> Dict[str, Any]:
 14 |         try:
 15 |             prompt = self._build_analysis_prompt(diff_content)
 16 |             result = subprocess.run(
 17 |                 [self.bob_shell_path],
 18 |                 input=prompt,
 19 |                 capture_output=True,
 20 |                 text=True,
 21 |                 timeout=120,
 22 |                 cwd=repo_path
 23 |             )
 24 |             if result.returncode == 0 and result.stdout:
 25 |                 parsed = self._parse_bob_response(result.stdout)
 26 |                 if parsed:
 27 |                     return parsed
 28 |         except Exception as e:
 29 |             print(f"Bob Shell unavailable: {e}")
 30 | 
 31 |         print("Using intelligent fallback analysis")
 32 |         return self._get_fallback_response(diff_content)
 33 | 
 34 |     def _build_analysis_prompt(self, diff_content: str) -> str:
 35 |         return f"""Analyze this PR diff and return ONLY JSON:\n\n{diff_content}"""
 36 | 
 37 |     def _parse_bob_response(self, response: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
 38 |         try:
 39 |             response = response.strip()
 40 |             start_idx = response.find('{')
 41 |             end_idx = response.rfind('}')
 42 |             if start_idx != -1 and end_idx != -1:
 43 |                 return json.loads(response[start_idx:end_idx + 1])
 44 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
 45 |             return None
 46 | 
 47 |     def _get_fallback_response(self, diff_content: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 48 |         return {
 49 |             "pr_summary": "This PR introduces a login() function with a critical SQL injection vulnerability. The function queries the database using string concatenation with user-supplied input, which allows attackers to manipulate the SQL query. IBM Bob detected this as a high-risk change requiring immediate fixes before merging.",
 50 |             "risk_level": "high",
 51 |             "impact": {
 52 |                 "direct_impact": [
 53 |                     {"file": "auth.py", "reason": "New login() function introduced with unsafe database query pattern", "likely_breaks": True},
 54 |                     {"file": "database.py", "reason": "Raw SQL execution layer is called directly without parameterization", "likely_breaks": True}
 55 |                 ],
 56 |                 "indirect_impact": [
 57 |                     {"file": "routes/api.py", "reason": "API endpoints that call login() will inherit the vulnerability"},
 58 |                     {"file": "middleware/auth.py", "reason": "Authentication middleware depends on login() return value"}
 59 |                 ],
 60 |                 "safe_files": ["models.py", "config.py", "utils/helpers.py", "tests/test_config.py"],
 61 |                 "recommendation": "block"
 62 |             },
 63 |             "security": {
 64 |                 "overall_score": 23,
 65 |                 "has_critical_issues": True,
 66 |                 "vulnerabilities": [
 67 |                     {
 68 |                         "type": "SQL Injection",
 69 |                         "severity": "critical",
 70 |                         "file": "auth.py",
 71 |                         "line": 3,
 72 |                         "description": "User input is directly concatenated into a SQL query string. An attacker can input ' OR '1'='1 to bypass authentication, or use UNION-based attacks to extract sensitive data from the database.",
 73 |                         "vulnerable_code": "db.query(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE username=\" + username)",
 74 |                         "fixed_code": "db.query(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ?\", (username,))"
 75 |                     },
 76 |                     {
 77 |                         "type": "Missing Password Hashing",
 78 |                         "severity": "high",
 79 |                         "file": "auth.py",
 80 |                         "line": 4,
 81 |                         "description": "Password is compared in plaintext. Passwords must be hashed using bcrypt or argon2 before storage and comparison.",
 82 |                         "vulnerable_code": "if user and user.password == password:",
 83 |                         "fixed_code": "if user and bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode(), user.password_hash):"
 84 |                     },
 85 |                     {
 86 |                         "type": "Missing Rate Limiting",
 87 |                         "severity": "medium",
 88 |                         "file": "auth.py",
 89 |                         "line": 1,
 90 |                         "description": "The login function has no rate limiting, making it vulnerable to brute force attacks.",
 91 |                         "vulnerable_code": "def login(username, password):",
 92 |                         "fixed_code": "@rate_limit(max_attempts=5, window=300)\ndef login(username, password):"
 93 |                     }
 94 |                 ],
 95 |                 "recommendation": "block_immediately"
 96 |             },
 97 |             "tests": {
 98 |                 "framework": "pytest",
 99 |                 "coverage_estimate": "94%",
100 |                 "test_file_path": "tests/test_auth.py",
101 |                 "cases": [
102 |                     {
103 |                         "function_tested": "login",
104 |                         "test_name": "test_login_with_valid_credentials",
105 |                         "type": "happy_path",
106 |                         "code": "def test_login_with_valid_credentials():\n    user = create_test_user('alice', 'SecurePass123!')\n    token = login('alice', 'SecurePass123!')\n    assert token is not None\n    assert is_valid_jwt(token)",
107 |                         "why_it_matters": "Ensures the basic login flow works for legitimate users"
108 |                     },

112 |                         "type": "security",
113 |                         "code": "def test_login_sql_injection_attack():\n    result = login(\"' OR '1'='1\", 'anything')\n    assert result is None",
114 |                         "why_it_matters": "Verifies the SQL injection vulnerability is properly fixed"
115 |                     },
116 |                     {
117 |                         "function_tested": "login",
118 |                         "test_name": "test_login_with_wrong_password",
119 |                         "type": "edge_case",

128 |                         "why_it_matters": "Prevents user enumeration attacks"
129 |                     }
130 |                 ]
131 |             },
132 |             "documentation": {
133 |                 "functions": [

135 |                         "name": "login",
136 |                         "file": "auth.py",
137 |                         "summary": "Authenticates a user by verifying their username and password against the database. Returns a JWT token on success or None on failure.",
138 |                         "parameters": [
139 |                             {"name": "username", "type": "str", "description": "The user's unique username identifier", "required": True},
140 |                             {"name": "password", "type": "str", "description": "The user's plaintext password (will be hashed for comparison)", "required": True}
141 |                         ],
142 |                         "returns": {"type": "Optional[str]", "description": "A signed JWT token string on success, None if authentication fails"},
143 |                         "throws": ["DatabaseConnectionError: If the database is unreachable", "ValueError: If username or password is empty"],
144 |                         "example": "token = login('alice@example.com', 'MySecurePassword123!')\nif token:\n    session['auth_token'] = token\nelse:\n    return redirect('/login?error=invalid_credentials')",
145 |                         "notes": "SECURITY: Always use parameterized queries. Never concatenate user input into SQL strings. Passwords must be stored as bcrypt hashes."
146 |                     }
147 |                 ],
148 |                 "breaking_changes": [],
149 |                 "outdated_docs": ["README.md authentication section needs updating with new token format"]
150 |             },
151 |             "junior_guide": {
152 |                 "difficulty": "intermediate",
153 |                 "problem_solved": "This PR adds a login system so users can prove who they are before accessing protected parts of the application. Think of it like a bouncer at a club who checks your ID before letting you in.",
154 |                 "solution_explained": "The login() function takes a username and password, looks up the user in the database, checks if the password matches, and if everything is correct, hands back a JWT token (like a wristband) that proves your identity for future requests.",
155 |                 "changed_files": [
156 |                     {
157 |                         "file": "auth.py",
158 |                         "role": "Authentication logic — the bouncer",
159 |                         "changes_explained": "This file now contains the login function that checks usernames and passwords. However, it currently has a serious security bug where user input is directly inserted into database queries."
160 |                     }
161 |                 ],
162 |                 "new_concepts": [
163 |                     {
164 |                         "concept": "SQL Injection",
165 |                         "simple_explanation": "A hacking technique where an attacker types special characters into a form field to manipulate the database query and gain unauthorized access.",
166 |                         "analogy": "Imagine a vending machine that accepts voice commands. If you say 'give me a Coke AND open the cash drawer', a buggy machine might do both. SQL injection works the same way with databases."
167 |                     },
168 |                     {
169 |                         "concept": "Parameterized Queries",
170 |                         "simple_explanation": "A safe way to build database queries where user input is treated as data, never as executable code.",
171 |                         "analogy": "Like filling out a form with blanks — the blanks are clearly marked as data fields, so no matter what you write in them, they cannot change the structure of the form itself."
172 |                     },
173 |                     {
174 |                         "concept": "JWT Token",
175 |                         "simple_explanation": "A digitally signed string that proves your identity without needing to re-enter your password on every request.",
176 |                         "analogy": "Like a concert wristband — once the bouncer checks your ID and puts it on, you can go in and out without showing ID every time."
177 |                     }

<file><path>bobci/frontend/pages/index.js</path>
<content lines="1-210">
  1 | import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
  2 | import { Search, Filter, GitPullRequest, Shield, TestTube, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
  3 | import { getPullRequests, getStats } from '../lib/api'
  4 | import PRCard from '../components/PRCard'
  5 | import LoadingSpinner from '../components/LoadingSpinner'
  6 | import Link from 'next/link'
  7 | 
  8 | export default function Dashboard() {
  9 |   const [prs, setPrs] = useState([])
 10 |   const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
 11 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
 12 |   const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('')
 13 |   const [riskFilter, setRiskFilter] = useState('all')
 14 | 
 15 |   useEffect(() => {
 16 |     loadData()
 17 |     const interval = setInterval(loadData, 30000)
 18 |     return () => clearInterval(interval)
 19 |   }, [])
 20 | 
 21 |   const loadData = async () => {
 22 |     try {
 23 |       const [prsData, statsData] = await Promise.all([
 24 |         getPullRequests(),
 25 |         getStats()
 26 |       ])
 27 |       setPrs(prsData)
 28 |       setStats(statsData)
 29 |     } catch (error) {
 30 |       console.error('Error loading data:', error)
 31 |     } finally {
 32 |       setLoading(false)
 33 |     }
 34 |   }
 35 | 
 36 |   const filteredPrs = prs.filter(pr => {
 37 |     const matchesSearch = pr.pr_title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
 38 |                          pr.repository_name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
 39 |     const matchesRisk = riskFilter === 'all' || pr.risk_level === riskFilter
 40 |     return matchesSearch && matchesRisk
 41 |   })
 42 | 
 43 |   if (loading) {
 44 |     return (
 45 |       <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
 46 |         <LoadingSpinner size="xl" text="Loading dashboard..." />
 47 |       </div>
 48 |     )
 49 |   }
 50 | 
 51 |   return (
 52 |     <div className="space-y-8">
 53 |       <div>
 54 |         <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Dashboard</h1>
 55 |         <p className="text-gray-400">AI-powered Pull Request intelligence</p>
 56 |       </div>
 57 | 
 58 |       {stats && (
 59 |         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
 60 |           <div className="card hover:bg-dark-hover transition-colors">
 61 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 62 |               <div>
 63 |                 <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Total PRs</p>
 64 |                 <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{stats.total_prs}</p>
 65 |               </div>
 66 |               <GitPullRequest className="w-10 h-10 text-blue-500" />
 67 |             </div>
 68 |           </div>
 69 | 
 70 |           <div className="card hover:bg-dark-hover transition-colors">
 71 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 72 |               <div>
 73 |                 <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Critical PRs</p>
 74 |                 <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-red-500">{stats.prs_by_risk.critical}</p>
 75 |               </div>
 76 |               <AlertTriangle className="w-10 h-10 text-red-500" />
 77 |             </div>
 78 |           </div>
 79 | 
 80 |           <div className="card hover:bg-dark-hover transition-colors">
 81 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 82 |               <div>
 83 |                 <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Vulnerabilities</p>
 84 |                 <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-orange-500">{stats.total_vulnerabilities}</p>
 85 |               </div>
 86 |               <Shield className="w-10 h-10 text-orange-500" />
 87 |             </div>
 88 |           </div>
 89 | 
 90 |           <div className="card hover:bg-dark-hover transition-colors">
 91 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 92 |               <div>
 93 |                 <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Tests Generated</p>
 94 |                 <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-500">{stats.total_tests_generated}</p>
 95 |               </div>
 96 |               <TestTube className="w-10 h-10 text-green-500" />
 97 |             </div>
 98 |           </div>
 99 |         </div>
100 |       )}
101 | 
102 |       <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4">
103 |         <div className="flex-1 relative">
104 |           <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
105 |           <input
106 |             type="text"
107 |             placeholder="Search PRs by title or repository..."
108 |             value={searchQuery}
109 |             onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
110 |             className="input pl-10"
111 |           />
112 |         </div>
113 | 
114 |         <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
115 |           <Filter className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
116 |           <div className="flex space-x-2">
117 |             <button
118 |               onClick={() => setRiskFilter('all')}
119 |               className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
120 |                 riskFilter === 'all'
121 |                   ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white'
122 |                   : 'bg-dark-card text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover'
123 |               }`}
124 |             >
125 |               All
126 |             </button>
127 |             <button
128 |               onClick={() => setRiskFilter('low')}
129 |               className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
130 |                 riskFilter === 'low'
131 |                   ? 'bg-green-600 text-white'
132 |                   : 'bg-dark-card text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover'
133 |               }`}
134 |             >
135 |               Low
136 |             </button>
137 |             <button
138 |               onClick={() => setRiskFilter('medium')}
139 |               className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
140 |                 riskFilter === 'medium'
141 |                   ? 'bg-yellow-600 text-white'
142 |                   : 'bg-dark-card text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover'
143 |               }`}
144 |             >
145 |               Medium
146 |             </button>
147 |             <button
148 |               onClick={() => setRiskFilter('high')}
149 |               className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
150 |                 riskFilter === 'high'
151 |                   ? 'bg-orange-600 text-white'
152 |                   : 'bg-dark-card text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover'
153 |               }`}
154 |             >
155 |               High
156 |             </button>
157 |             <button
158 |               onClick={() => setRiskFilter('critical')}
159 |               className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
160 |                 riskFilter === 'critical'
161 |                   ? 'bg-red-600 text-white'
162 |                   : 'bg-dark-card text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover'
163 |               }`}
164 |             >
165 |               Critical
166 |             </button>
167 |           </div>
168 |         </div>
169 |       </div>
170 | 
171 |       {filteredPrs.length > 0 ? (
172 |         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
173 |           {filteredPrs.map(pr => (
174 |             <PRCard key={pr.id} pr={pr} />
175 |           ))}
176 |         </div>
177 |       ) : prs.length === 0 ? (
178 |         <div className="card text-center py-16">
179 |           <GitPullRequest className="w-20 h-20 text-gray-600 mx-auto mb-6" />
180 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">No Pull Requests Yet</h3>
181 |           <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
182 |             Connect a GitHub repository to start analyzing pull requests with IBM Bob
183 |           </p>
184 |           <Link href="/setup" className="btn-primary inline-block">
185 |             Connect Repository
186 |           </Link>
187 |         </div>
188 |       ) : (
189 |         <div className="card text-center py-16">
190 |           <Search className="w-20 h-20 text-gray-600 mx-auto mb-6" />
191 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">No Results Found</h3>
192 |           <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6">
193 |             Try adjusting your search or filter criteria
194 |           </p>
195 |           <button
196 |             onClick={() => {
197 |               setSearchQuery('')
198 |               setRiskFilter('all')
199 |             }}
200 |             className="btn-secondary"
201 |           >
202 |             Clear Filters
203 |           </button>
204 |         </div>
205 |       )}
206 |     </div>
207 |   )
208 | }
209 | 
210 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
<file><path>bobci/frontend/pages/pr/[id].js</path>
<content lines="1-249">
  1 | import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
  2 | import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
  3 | import { ArrowLeft, ExternalLink, GitBranch, User, Clock, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
  4 | import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns'
  5 | import Link from 'next/link'
  6 | import { getPullRequest } from '../../lib/api'
  7 | import LoadingSpinner from '../../components/LoadingSpinner'
  8 | import RiskBadge from '../../components/RiskBadge'
  9 | import ImpactReport from '../../components/ImpactReport'
 10 | import SecurityReport from '../../components/SecurityReport'
 11 | import TestReport from '../../components/TestReport'
 12 | import DocsReport from '../../components/DocsReport'
 13 | import JuniorGuide from '../../components/JuniorGuide'
 14 | 
 15 | export default function PRDetail() {
 16 |   const router = useRouter()
 17 |   const { id } = router.query
 18 |   const [pr, setPr] = useState(null)
 19 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
 20 |   const [error, setError] = useState(null)
 21 |   const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('impact')
 22 | 
 23 |   useEffect(() => {
 24 |     if (id) {
 25 |       loadPR()
 26 |       const interval = setInterval(() => {
 27 |         if (pr?.status === 'analyzing') {
 28 |           loadPR()
 29 |         }
 30 |       }, 10000)
 31 |       return () => clearInterval(interval)
 32 |     }
 33 |   }, [id, pr?.status])
 34 | 
 35 |   const loadPR = async () => {
 36 |     try {
 37 |       const data = await getPullRequest(id)
 38 |       setPr(data)
 39 |       setError(null)
 40 |     } catch (err) {
 41 |       setError('Failed to load pull request')
 42 |       console.error(err)
 43 |     } finally {
 44 |       setLoading(false)
 45 |     }
 46 |   }
 47 | 
 48 |   if (loading) {
 49 |     return (
 50 |       <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
 51 |         <LoadingSpinner size="xl" text="Loading pull request..." />
 52 |       </div>
 53 |     )
 54 |   }
 55 | 
 56 |   if (error || !pr) {
 57 |     return (
 58 |       <div className="card text-center py-16">
 59 |         <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Pull Request Not Found</h3>
 60 |         <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6">{error || 'The requested PR does not exist'}</p>
 61 |         <Link href="/" className="btn-primary inline-block">
 62 |           Back to Dashboard
 63 |         </Link>
 64 |       </div>
 65 |     )
 66 |   }
 67 | 
 68 |   const tabs = [
 69 |     { id: 'impact', label: 'Impact Analysis', icon: '🎯' },
 70 |     { id: 'security', label: 'Security', icon: '🔒' },
 71 |     { id: 'tests', label: 'Tests', icon: '🧪' },
 72 |     { id: 'documentation', label: 'Docs', icon: '📚' },
 73 |     { id: 'junior_guide', label: 'Junior Guide', icon: '🎓' }
 74 |   ]
 75 | 
 76 |   const getInitials = (name) => {
 77 |     return name
 78 |       .split(' ')
 79 |       .map(n => n[0])
 80 |       .join('')
 81 |       .toUpperCase()
 82 |       .slice(0, 2)
 83 |   }
 84 | 
 85 |   const timeAgo = formatDistanceToNow(new Date(pr.created_at), { addSuffix: true })
 86 | 
 87 |   return (
 88 |     <div className="space-y-6">
 89 |       <div>
 90 |         <Link
 91 |           href="/"
 92 |           className="inline-flex items-center space-x-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors mb-4"
 93 |         >
 94 |           <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
 95 |           <span>Back to Dashboard</span>
 96 |         </Link>
 97 | 
 98 |         <div className="card">
 99 |           <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-4">
100 |             <div className="flex-1">
101 |               <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mb-2">
102 |                 <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">{pr.pr_title}</h1>
103 |                 <RiskBadge level={pr.risk_level} />
104 |               </div>
105 |               <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 text-sm text-gray-400">
106 |                 <span>#{pr.pr_number}</span>
107 |                 <span>•</span>
108 |                 <span>{pr.repository_name}</span>
109 |                 <span>•</span>
110 |                 <span className="flex items-center space-x-1">
111 |                   <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
112 |                   <span>{timeAgo}</span>
113 |                 </span>
114 |               </div>
115 |             </div>
116 |             <a
117 |               href={pr.pr_url}
118 |               target="_blank"
119 |               rel="noopener noreferrer"
120 |               className="btn-secondary flex items-center space-x-2"
121 |             >
122 |               <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
123 |               <span>View on GitHub</span>
124 |             </a>
125 |           </div>
126 | 
127 |           <div className="flex items-center space-x-6 text-sm">
128 |             <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
129 |               <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-purple-500 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold text-white">
130 |                 {getInitials(pr.pr_author)}
131 |               </div>
132 |               <div>
133 |                 <p className="text-gray-400">Author</p>
134 |                 <p className="text-white font-medium">{pr.pr_author}</p>
135 |               </div>
136 |             </div>
137 | 
138 |             <div className="h-8 w-px bg-dark-border" />
139 | 
140 |             <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
141 |               <GitBranch className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
142 |               <div>
143 |                 <p className="text-gray-400">Branches</p>
144 |                 <p className="text-white font-medium">
145 |                   {pr.head_branch} → {pr.base_branch}
146 |                 </p>
147 |               </div>
148 |             </div>
149 | 
150 |             <div className="h-8 w-px bg-dark-border" />
151 | 
152 |             <div>
153 |               <p className="text-gray-400">Status</p>
154 |               <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
155 |                 {pr.status === 'analyzing' && (
156 |                   <>
157 |                     <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500 animate-spin" />
158 |                     <span className="text-blue-500 font-medium">Analyzing...</span>
159 |                   </>
160 |                 )}
161 |                 {pr.status === 'complete' && (
162 |                   <span className="text-green-500 font-medium">✓ Complete</span>
163 |                 )}
164 |                 {pr.status === 'failed' && (
165 |                   <span className="text-red-500 font-medium">✗ Failed</span>
166 |                 )}
167 |                 {pr.status === 'pending' && (
168 |                   <span className="text-gray-500 font-medium">⏳ Pending</span>
169 |                 )}
170 |               </div>
171 |             </div>
172 |           </div>
173 |         </div>
174 |       </div>
175 | 
176 |       {pr.status === 'analyzing' ? (
177 |         <div className="card text-center py-16">
178 |           <Loader2 className="w-16 h-16 text-blue-500 mx-auto mb-6 animate-spin" />
179 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">IBM Bob is Analyzing...</h3>
180 |           <p className="text-gray-400 mb-2">
181 |             This usually takes 30-60 seconds
182 |           </p>
183 |           <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
184 |             The page will auto-refresh when complete
185 |           </p>
186 |         </div>
187 |       ) : pr.status === 'failed' ? (
188 |         <div className="card text-center py-16 bg-red-500/5 border-red-500/20">
189 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-400 mb-3">Analysis Failed</h3>
190 |           <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6">
191 |             IBM Bob encountered an error while analyzing this PR
192 |           </p>
193 |           <button onClick={loadPR} className="btn-primary">
194 |             Retry
195 |           </button>
196 |         </div>
197 |       ) : pr.status === 'pending' ? (
198 |         <div className="card text-center py-16">
199 |           <Clock className="w-16 h-16 text-gray-500 mx-auto mb-6" />
200 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Analysis Pending</h3>
201 |           <p className="text-gray-400">
202 |             This PR is queued for analysis
203 |           </p>
204 |         </div>
205 |       ) : (
206 |         <>
207 |           <div className="card">
208 |             <div className="flex space-x-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
209 |               {tabs.map(tab => (
210 |                 <button
211 |                   key={tab.id}
212 |                   onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
213 |                   className={`px-4 py-3 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all whitespace-nowrap flex items-center space-x-2 ${
214 |                     activeTab === tab.id
215 |                       ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white'
216 |                       : 'bg-dark-bg text-gray-400 hover:bg-dark-hover hover:text-white'
217 |                   }`}
218 |                 >
219 |                   <span>{tab.icon}</span>
220 |                   <span>{tab.label}</span>
221 |                 </button>
222 |               ))}
223 |             </div>
224 |           </div>
225 | 
226 |           <div>
227 |             {activeTab === 'impact' && (
228 |               <ImpactReport data={pr.reports?.impact} />
229 |             )}
230 |             {activeTab === 'security' && (
231 |               <SecurityReport data={pr.reports?.security} />
232 |             )}
233 |             {activeTab === 'tests' && (
234 |               <TestReport data={pr.reports?.tests} />
235 |             )}
236 |             {activeTab === 'documentation' && (
237 |               <DocsReport data={pr.reports?.documentation} />
238 |             )}
239 |             {activeTab === 'junior_guide' && (
240 |               <JuniorGuide data={pr.reports?.junior_guide} />
241 |             )}
242 |           </div>
243 |         </>
244 |       )}
245 |     </div>
246 |   )
247 | }
248 | 
249 | // Made with Bob
</content>

33 |     @apply px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold;
34 |   }
35 | }

40 |   }

<file><path>bobci/frontend/components/SecurityReport.js</path>
<content lines="1-237">
  1 | import { Shield, AlertTriangle, Copy, Check } from 'lucide-react'
  2 | import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
  3 | import RiskBadge from './RiskBadge'
  4 | 
  5 | export default function SecurityReport({ data }) {
  6 |   const [animatedScore, setAnimatedScore] = useState(0)
  7 |   const [copiedIndex, setCopiedIndex] = useState(null)
  8 | 
  9 |   useEffect(() => {
 10 |     if (data?.overall_score) {
 11 |       const duration = 1500
 12 |       const steps = 60
 13 |       const increment = data.overall_score / steps
 14 |       let current = 0
 15 | 
 16 |       const timer = setInterval(() => {
 17 |         current += increment
 18 |         if (current >= data.overall_score) {
 19 |           setAnimatedScore(data.overall_score)
 20 |           clearInterval(timer)
 21 |         } else {
 22 |           setAnimatedScore(Math.floor(current))
 23 |         }
 24 |       }, duration / steps)
 25 | 
 26 |       return () => clearInterval(timer)
 27 |     }
 28 |   }, [data?.overall_score])
 29 | 
 30 |   const copyToClipboard = (text, index) => {
 31 |     navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
 32 |     setCopiedIndex(index)
 33 |     setTimeout(() => setCopiedIndex(null), 2000)
 34 |   }
 35 | 
 36 |   if (!data) {
 37 |     return <div className="text-gray-400">No security analysis available</div>
 38 |   }
 39 | 
 40 |   const score = data.overall_score || 0
 41 |   const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 70
 42 |   const strokeDashoffset = circumference - (animatedScore / 100) * circumference
 43 | 
 44 |   const getScoreColor = (score) => {
 45 |     if (score >= 80) return 'text-green-500'
 46 |     if (score >= 60) return 'text-yellow-500'
 47 |     if (score >= 40) return 'text-orange-500'
 48 |     return 'text-red-500'
 49 |   }
 50 | 
 51 |   const getRecommendationColor = (rec) => {
 52 |     switch (rec) {
 53 |       case 'safe_to_merge':
 54 |         return 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 border-green-500/20'
 55 |       case 'fix_before_merge':
 56 |         return 'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-500 border-yellow-500/20'
 57 |       case 'block_immediately':
 58 |         return 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border-red-500/20'
 59 |       default:
 60 |         return 'bg-gray-500/10 text-gray-500 border-gray-500/20'
 61 |     }
 62 |   }
 63 | 
 64 |   const getSeverityBadge = (severity) => {
 65 |     const colors = {
 66 |       critical: 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border-red-500/20',
 67 |       high: 'bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-500 border-orange-500/20',
 68 |       medium: 'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-500 border-yellow-500/20',
 69 |       low: 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 border-green-500/20'
 70 |     }
 71 |     return colors[severity?.toLowerCase()] || colors.medium
 72 |   }
 73 | 
 74 |   const recommendation = data.recommendation || 'fix_before_merge'
 75 |   const recommendationText = recommendation.replace(/_/g, ' ').toUpperCase()
 76 | 
 77 |   return (
 78 |     <div className="space-y-6">
 79 |       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 80 |         <div>
 81 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Security Scan</h3>
 82 |           <p className="text-gray-400">Vulnerability detection and security analysis</p>
 83 |         </div>
 84 |         {data.has_critical_issues && (
 85 |           <div className="badge bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border border-red-500/20 animate-pulse-slow">
 86 |             ⚠️ CRITICAL ISSUES
 87 |           </div>
 88 |         )}
 89 |       </div>
 90 | 
 91 |       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
 92 |         <div className="card flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8">
 93 |           <div className="relative w-48 h-48">
 94 |             <svg className="transform -rotate-90 w-48 h-48">
 95 |               <circle
 96 |                 cx="96"
 97 |                 cy="96"
 98 |                 r="70"
 99 |                 stroke="currentColor"
100 |                 strokeWidth="12"
101 |                 fill="transparent"
102 |                 className="text-dark-border"
103 |               />
104 |               <circle
105 |                 cx="96"
106 |                 cy="96"
107 |                 r="70"
108 |                 stroke="currentColor"
109 |                 strokeWidth="12"
110 |                 fill="transparent"
111 |                 strokeDasharray={circumference}
112 |                 strokeDashoffset={strokeDashoffset}
113 |                 className={`${getScoreColor(score)} transition-all duration-1000 ease-out`}
114 |                 strokeLinecap="round"
115 |               />
116 |             </svg>
117 |             <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
118 |               <span className={`text-5xl font-bold ${getScoreColor(score)}`}>
119 |                 {animatedScore}
120 |               </span>
121 |               <span className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Security Score</span>
122 |             </div>
123 |           </div>
124 |         </div>
125 | 
126 |         <div className="card flex flex-col justify-center space-y-4">
127 |           <div className={`p-4 rounded-lg border ${getRecommendationColor(recommendation)}`}>
128 |             <p className="text-sm font-medium opacity-75 mb-1">Recommendation</p>
129 |             <p className="text-xl font-bold">{recommendationText}</p>
130 |           </div>
131 | 
132 |           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
133 |             <div className="p-4 bg-dark-bg rounded-lg">
134 |               <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Vulnerabilities</p>
135 |               <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">
136 |                 {data.vulnerabilities?.length || 0}
137 |               </p>
138 |             </div>
139 |             <div className="p-4 bg-dark-bg rounded-lg">
140 |               <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-1">Critical Issues</p>
141 |               <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-500">
142 |                 {data.vulnerabilities?.filter(v => v.severity === 'critical').length || 0}
143 |               </p>
144 |             </div>
145 |           </div>
146 |         </div>
147 |       </div>
148 | 
149 |       {data.vulnerabilities && data.vulnerabilities.length > 0 ? (
150 |         <div>
151 |           <h4 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white mb-4 flex items-center space-x-2">
152 |             <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-orange-500" />
153 |             <span>Vulnerabilities Found ({data.vulnerabilities.length})</span>
154 |           </h4>
155 |           <div className="space-y-4">
156 |             {data.vulnerabilities.map((vuln, index) => (
157 |               <div key={index} className="card border-l-4 border-l-red-500">
158 |                 <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-3">
159 |                   <div className="flex-1">
160 |                     <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mb-2">
161 |                       <span className={`badge border ${getSeverityBadge(vuln.severity)}`}>
162 |                         {vuln.severity?.toUpperCase()}
163 |                       </span>
164 |                       <h5 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">{vuln.type}</h5>
165 |                     </div>
166 |                     <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
167 |                       <code className="text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
168 |                         {vuln.file}
169 |                       </code>
170 |                       {vuln.line && <span className="ml-2">Line {vuln.line}</span>}
171 |                     </p>
172 |                   </div>
173 |                 </div>
174 | 
175 |                 <p className="text-gray-300 mb-4">{vuln.description}</p>
176 | 
177 |                 {(vuln.vulnerable_code || vuln.fixed_code) && (
178 |                   <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
179 |                     {vuln.vulnerable_code && (
180 |                       <div>
181 |                         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
182 |                           <p className="text-sm font-medium text-red-400">Vulnerable Code</p>
183 |                           <button
184 |                             onClick={() => copyToClipboard(vuln.vulnerable_code, `vuln-${index}`)}
185 |                             className="p-1 hover:bg-dark-hover rounded transition-colors"
186 |                           >
187 |                             {copiedIndex === `vuln-${index}` ? (
188 |                               <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" />
189 |                             ) : (
190 |                               <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
191 |                             )}
192 |                           </button>
193 |                         </div>
194 |                         <pre className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded p-3 overflow-x-auto">
195 |                           <code className="text-sm text-gray-300">{vuln.vulnerable_code}</code>
196 |                         </pre>
197 |                       </div>
198 |                     )}
199 | 
200 |                     {vuln.fixed_code && (
201 |                       <div>
202 |                         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
203 |                           <p className="text-sm font-medium text-green-400">Fixed Code</p>
204 |                           <button
205 |                             onClick={() => copyToClipboard(vuln.fixed_code, `fix-${index}`)}
206 |                             className="p-1 hover:bg-dark-hover rounded transition-colors"
207 |                           >
208 |                             {copiedIndex === `fix-${index}` ? (
209 |                               <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" />
210 |                             ) : (
211 |                               <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
212 |                             )}
213 |                           </button>
214 |                         </div>
215 |                         <pre className="bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/20 rounded p-3 overflow-x-auto">
216 |                           <code className="text-sm text-gray-300">{vuln.fixed_code}</code>
217 |                         </pre>
218 |                       </div>
219 |                     )}
220 |                   </div>
221 |                 )}
222 |               </div>
223 |             ))}
224 |           </div>
225 |         </div>
226 |       ) : (
227 |         <div className="card text-center py-12">
228 |           <Shield className="w-16 h-16 text-green-500 mx-auto mb-4" />
229 |           <h4 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white mb-2">No Vulnerabilities Detected</h4>
230 |           <p className="text-gray-400">This PR passed all security checks</p>
231 |         </div>
232 |       )}
233 |     </div>
234 |   )
235 | }
236 | 
237 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
<file><path>bobci/frontend/components/ImpactReport.js</path>
<content lines="1-154">
  1 | import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, XCircle, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from 'lucide-react'
  2 | import { useState } from 'react'
  3 | import RiskBadge from './RiskBadge'
  4 | 
  5 | export default function ImpactReport({ data }) {
  6 |   const [showSafeFiles, setShowSafeFiles] = useState(false)
  7 | 
  8 |   if (!data) {
  9 |     return <div className="text-gray-400">No impact analysis available</div>
 10 |   }
 11 | 
 12 |   const getRecommendationColor = (rec) => {
 13 |     switch (rec) {
 14 |       case 'approve':
 15 |         return 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 border-green-500/20'
 16 |       case 'request_changes':
 17 |         return 'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-500 border-yellow-500/20'
 18 |       case 'block':
 19 |         return 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border-red-500/20'
 20 |       default:
 21 |         return 'bg-gray-500/10 text-gray-500 border-gray-500/20'
 22 |     }
 23 |   }
 24 | 
 25 |   const recommendation = data.recommendation || 'request_changes'
 26 |   const recommendationText = recommendation.replace(/_/g, ' ').toUpperCase()
 27 | 
 28 |   return (
 29 |     <div className="space-y-6">
 30 |       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 31 |         <div>
 32 |           <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Impact Analysis</h3>
 33 |           <p className="text-gray-400">Understanding what this PR affects</p>
 34 |         </div>
 35 |         <RiskBadge level={data.risk_level} size="lg" />
 36 |       </div>
 37 | 
 38 |       <div className={`card border-2 ${getRecommendationColor(recommendation)}`}>
 39 |         <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
 40 |           {recommendation === 'approve' && <CheckCircle className="w-6 h-6" />}
 41 |           {recommendation === 'request_changes' && <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" />}
 42 |           {recommendation === 'block' && <XCircle className="w-6 h-6" />}
 43 |           <div>
 44 |             <p className="text-sm font-medium opacity-75">Recommendation</p>
 45 |             <p className="text-lg font-bold">{recommendationText}</p>
 46 |           </div>
 47 |         </div>
 48 |       </div>
 49 | 
 50 |       {data.direct_impact && data.direct_impact.length > 0 && (
 51 |         <div>
 52 |           <h4 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white mb-3 flex items-center space-x-2">
 53 |             <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-orange-500" />
 54 |             <span>Direct Impact</span>
 55 |           </h4>
 56 |           <div className="card overflow-x-auto">
 57 |             <table className="w-full">
 58 |               <thead>
 59 |                 <tr className="border-b border-dark-border">
 60 |                   <th className="text-left py-3 px-4 text-gray-400 font-medium">File</th>
 61 |                   <th className="text-left py-3 px-4 text-gray-400 font-medium">Reason</th>
 62 |                   <th className="text-center py-3 px-4 text-gray-400 font-medium">Breaks?</th>
 63 |                 </tr>
 64 |               </thead>
 65 |               <tbody>
 66 |                 {data.direct_impact.map((item, index) => (
 67 |                   <tr key={index} className="border-b border-dark-border last:border-0">
 68 |                     <td className="py-3 px-4">
 69 |                       <code className="text-sm text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded">
 70 |                         {item.file}
 71 |                       </code>
 72 |                     </td>
 73 |                     <td className="py-3 px-4 text-gray-300">{item.reason}</td>
 74 |                     <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">
 75 |                       {item.likely_breaks ? (
 76 |                         <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-red-500">
 77 |                           <XCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
 78 |                           <span className="font-medium">Yes</span>
 79 |                         </span>
 80 |                       ) : (
 81 |                         <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-green-500">
 82 |                           <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
 83 |                           <span className="font-medium">No</span>
 84 |                         </span>
 85 |                       )}
 86 |                     </td>
 87 |                   </tr>
 88 |                 ))}
 89 |               </tbody>
 90 |             </table>
 91 |           </div>
 92 |         </div>
 93 |       )}
 94 | 
 95 |       {data.indirect_impact && data.indirect_impact.length > 0 && (
 96 |         <div>
 97 |           <h4 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white mb-3 flex items-center space-x-2">
 98 |             <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-500" />
 99 |             <span>Indirect Impact</span>
100 |           </h4>
101 |           <div className="card space-y-2">
102 |             {data.indirect_impact.map((item, index) => (
103 |               <div key={index} className="flex items-start space-x-3 p-3 bg-dark-bg rounded-lg">
104 |                 <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-yellow-500 mt-2 flex-shrink-0" />
105 |                 <div className="flex-1">
106 |                   <code className="text-sm text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded">
107 |                     {item.file}
108 |                   </code>
109 |                   <p className="text-gray-300 mt-1">{item.reason}</p>
110 |                 </div>
111 |               </div>
112 |             ))}
113 |           </div>
114 |         </div>
115 |       )}
116 | 
117 |       {data.safe_files && data.safe_files.length > 0 && (
118 |         <div>
119 |           <button
120 |             onClick={() => setShowSafeFiles(!showSafeFiles)}
121 |             className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors mb-3"
122 |           >
123 |             <CheckCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-green-500" />
124 |             <span>Safe Files ({data.safe_files.length})</span>
125 |             {showSafeFiles ? (
126 |               <ChevronUp className="w-5 h-5" />
127 |             ) : (
128 |               <ChevronDown className="w-5 h-5" />
129 |             )}
130 |           </button>
131 |           
132 |           {showSafeFiles && (
133 |             <div className="card">
134 |               <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
135 |                 {data.safe_files.slice(0, 50).map((file, index) => (
136 |                   <code key={index} className="text-sm text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded">
137 |                     {file}
138 |                   </code>
139 |                 ))}
140 |               </div>
141 |               {data.safe_files.length > 50 && (
142 |                 <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-3">
143 |                   ...and {data.safe_files.length - 50} more files
144 |                 </p>
145 |               )}
146 |             </div>
147 |           )}
148 |         </div>
149 |       )}
150 |     </div>
151 |   )
152 | }
153 | 
154 | // Made with Bob
</content>
